## Closest call: wagtail-localize#967 (rejected — maintainer AI-scrutiny signal)
- Issue qualifies on paper: COLLABORATOR-filed (zerolab), `help wanted` + `good first issue`, unassigned, repo stars 257 ≥ 100, no AI_USAGE_POLICY.md file. Fix PR #968 (NONE 1cbyc, `Fixes #967`) closed unmerged 11-04 — claim freed, so full re-vet was warranted.
- Maintainer verdict on the PR (MEMBER Stormheg, review + inline comment, read this cycle): "We are mocking the implementation, which I don't find acceptable" and "This PR doesn't disclose AI usage per the PR template, but I suspect it was used… Since Wagtail receives a lot of AI-generated contributions these days, we are a lot stricter about how we spend our time reviewing these. Since this is a fairly trivial change, I think it is better if we close this."
- Reject rationale (double-grounded): (1) repo AI posture is now explicit in practice — stricter review of AI-suspected contributions with non-disclosure treated as close-worthy; my contribution would be AI-assisted by definition (disclosure mandatory), entering a queue the maintainer just said they are stricter about, on the exact issue closed yesterday; (2) "better if we close this" signals no appetite for another external pass at a trivial change. Any-doubt = skip. Not skipped lightly — full issue/PR/review read.
- Note for future cycles: treat wagtail org as AI-sensitive; any wagtail candidate needs a clearly non-trivial, human-driven angle or maintainer invitation, else skip.

## Implementation (cycle 57 — /tmp/rc-sdk clean workspace, default branch `mobile` ecf4fed)
- Baseline: `tsc -p tsconfig.json` exit 0; close+open suites 61 pass. Scratch repro of the issue's exact steps (3 variants) showed the hang itself already fixed via #256 + Connection rewrite — remaining ask per the issue ("nothing in the current specs pins what happens to them") is pinning specs.
- Fix: 3 pinning specs in `lib/drivers/__tests__/socket.close.spec.ts` (+69/-1, 1 file): close asks a CONNECTING transport to close with the intentional code and settles on its close event; close after pre-handshake transport death settles with no reopen and idle driver; late open after deadline-answered close emits/writes nothing.
- Failing-test-first proof: temporarily restored the old skip-the-wait behavior — new test 1 fails (`closedWith` never receives the intentional close); restored, all green.
- Verification gate: full suite 453 tests / 34 suites pass; `typecheck` clean on all three tsconfigs; `oxlint` only pre-existing warnings in untouched `lib/api/api.ts`. Critic 0.85: pass (behavior-pinning tests using existing helpers, file-style names, no tautologies — each asserts cross-boundary effects). Ravan security: pass (test-only diff, no new surface, no secrets/network).
- Submit: intent comment posted on #295; forked; exact reviewed commit a054e89 pushed to fork branch `test/295-close-connecting-pins`; PR https://github.com/RocketChat/Rocket.Chat.js.SDK/pull/438 opened against `mobile` with disclosure line. SHAs verified equal. Submitted today now 1/2.
